# Depth expansion coverage matrix

Traceability matrix for `docs/spec/13-depth-expansion.md` (spec J.19). Generated and checked by `scripts/spec_coverage.py`.
Every numbered item of the spec has one row.
A row is `done` only when every work column is filled (or `n/a` where it does not apply) and its test passes.
`deferred-approved` requires the user's explicit approval and a reason in the Test column.
Gap rows (kind `gap`) are done when every section they point to is done.
Run `python scripts/spec_coverage.py write` after editing the spec, and `python scripts/spec_coverage.py check` to verify.

milestones give hope and conviction, fulfilment a formative pride, a maimed body or a dead patron shatters a dream into despair unless cheer and perseverance re-dream; wants come from pressing needs and the dream, steer the calling chosen (mind_skills) and the chance of a match (mortal_kin), set out for a neighbouring community and swear pilgrimages (treaty_cycles), and fade unmet; fears come from facets and children and darken the mood when war, plague, beasts, wounds, a child in danger, poverty, shame or the dark come near; office holders and the highly skilled take a focus that speeds its skills and unlocks perk modifiers; elated mortals are seized by a masterwork fever (spends a material, makes a named jewel of very high quality, or leaves visions), a research insight (a people's research jumps) or a heroic resolve (no fear, stronger in duels) | aspiration:dream, aspiration:dream_remove, aspiration:want, aspiration:want_edit, aspiration:want_remove, aspiration:fear, aspiration:fear_remove, aspiration:focus, aspiration:perk, aspiration:perk_remove, aspiration:inspire, aspiration:inspiration_edit, aspiration:inspiration_remove, mind:lock, mind:unlock | AspirationsSection in ThingSections (dream card with milestones, wants, fears, focus and perks, inspirations, controls with holds and value editors) | aspiration_lines: the dream, wants as suggested objectives, fears, focus, inspiration | inspiration.masterwork, aspiration.journey | migration f8b0d2e4a6c7: adults take up dreams, wants and fears from the next epoch | tests/engine/mind/test_aspirations.py; | F21 | foundation | Communication: correspondence, envoys, public opinion and etiquette | §F21 | domain/enums/communication.py, catalog/communication.py, db/tables/lore.py (messages gain medium, content, state, language, envoy and fate, interceptor, payload, arrival, forged, misread, enciphered), db/tables/communication.py (public_opinion), engine/communication/{post,dispatches,opinion}.py, OfficeKind.HERALD with the proclaim power, LawKind.CENSORSHIP; hooks: declare_war sends a herald with the declaration, accords.propose sends the proposal by envoy, spy networks report as intelligence that becomes belief on arrival, god visions travel by dream, the forge_letter scheme sends a forged declaration in a realm's name, legitimacy, stability, war weariness and law enforcement read public opinion, hierarchical realms out of favour enact censorship | correspondence: every message takes epochs by regions crossed and medium speed, longer over land in the cold season, between worlds a fixed journey, shorter along its sender's signal links; spy networks watching either end intercept it (reading it unless enciphered, forging a replacement or, when at war with the sender, seizing it), outlaws at either end waylay messengers over land; on arrival a message between tongues may be misread by its clauses against the recipient's skill in languages, reports and intelligence become beliefs, a forged declaration starts a war against the named sender, insults and threats sting (twice in honour cultures, by herald twice and by letter half in face cultures), gifts are valued by the recipient culture's tastes and leave a favour, a misread proposal becomes a treaty dispute, and no realm answers a proposal before it arrives, so a peace may come after the battle; envoys carry the sender's negotiation and etiquette and may be insulted, imprisoned or slain by a hostile court, which brings war; rulers warm to a realm send gifts and rulers cold to one send insults; public_opinion: every settlement and people holds an approval of its ruler, its realm's wars, its laws, its gods and its creeds, drifting toward what its fortune, unrest, the ruler's renown, heralds, rumours (amplified by printing, muted by censorship) and art suggest; noticed censorship sours approval | dispatch:send, dispatch:edit, dispatch:hold, dispatch:delete, opinion:set, opinion:delete | CorrespondenceSection in ThingSections (KPI tiles; letters received, sent and intercepted with medium, state, arrival, cipher, forgery, misreading and the envoy's fate, editors and send form; | F41 | foundation | Purity and pollution | §F41 | db/tables/folkways.py (purity, impurity_sources, purifications, purity_ranks); engine/folkways/purity.py; cultures that value purity hold their own sources of impurity and ways of purification, those that also value hierarchy rank their folk in hereditary castes; touching a source (the dead, birth, fluids, food, species, professions, outsiders, places, objects, acts, made things, taboos, wounds) makes a mortal impure or defiled, the impure are shut out of gatherings and unions, purification costs coin and may fail, ranks pass to children, bar marriage across ranks and the professions of other ranks, and wealth lets a mortal rise; the unclean may not work rites; a people that shuns the dead and the sick slows an epidemic but leaves its sick without carers, and those unclean from handling the dead die of plague more often; a people holding outsiders unclean trades with strangers only at a trading post; each caste of a town holds its quarter, as desirable as the caste's standing | every epoch: purity in the mind stage | purity:set/edit/delete/cleanse, impurity_source:set/edit/delete, purification:set/edit/delete, purity_rank:create/edit/delete/profession | PurityPanel (chart-purity) in section-folkways | purity and rank in the mortal context; declare_impurity (son), purify_self (mortal) | none | migration 4eb7d40871b5: cultures hold their purity rules from the next epoch | tests/engine/core/test_folkways_w28.py::test_a_culture_of_purity_shuts_the_impure_out_of_unions_until_they_purify; tests/engine/core/test_folkways_w28.py::test_a_caste_rank_passes_to_children_and_bars_the_work_of_other_ranks; tests/engine/core/test_folkways_w28.py::test_the_unclean_are_kept_from_rites_and_a_caste_town_gives_each_caste_its_quarter; tests/engine/core/test_folkways_w28.py::test_a_people_that_holds_outsiders_unclean_trades_with_strangers_only_at_a_trading_post; folkways.test.tsx; city.test.tsx | done |

charms are real where enchanted, blessed or spirit-bound and calm their bearers either way; misfortune read as witchcraft brings an accusation chosen by envy, outsiders, isolation and keeping apart, grudges, rumours the town carries and true castings, or named by diviners, truly where their reading is true, tried by the courts as the crime of witchcraft; | F43 | foundation | Identity layers and salience | §F43 | db/tables/belonging.py (held_identities); engine/belonging/identities.py, values.py, effects.py; every town, people and named mortal holds identities in nine layers (people, faith, rank, home place, house, craft or calling, generation, realm, circle) with a value, how firmly it is held and how much it matters; a realm's holy war raises faith, a year of hunger rank, an enemy army in the land people and realm, a strike craft, a movement's preaching faith or circle, a foreign quarter, kept-apart ranks or the quarters of castes people and rank, and marriages and guilds across peoples or faiths lower those lines, each drifting back to rest; a mortal takes most of its home's salience; holders sharing salient identities think better of each other (an opinion term beside the old shared god and tongue); a realm's most salient layer is the line it divides along, so a member that differs from its ruler there drifts from its loyalty and its crowds rise more readily, while a salient bond with the realm holds it and raises its levy; the salient house, guild, circle or home of a place keeps its own account of what happened there, glorifying its own | every epoch: belonging in the mind stage; opinion reads shared identity; realm loyalty reads cohesion and separatism; the levy reads realm salience; historiography lets identity groups keep their accounts | held_identity:set/edit/delete, belonging:run | IdentityPanel (chart-identities) in section-belonging of BelongingSection; | F44 | foundation | The technology ceiling: advanced eras | §F44 | catalog/techniques.py, catalog/eras.py; db/tables/eras.py (tech_programmes); engine/eras/effects.py, progress.py; thirty-one techniques of the gunpowder, steam, electrical, information and advanced eras on the same prerequisites, branch levels and gates as every technique, each moving typed channels (harvests, mining, industry, sickness spared, message reach, army march, sea routes, routes between worlds, walls, pollution, speed of opinion, research, secularisation, missile strength, power rites, spoilage, doom) through ruled amounts; the era of a thing is read from what its town, people, home or realm's capital knows; factories raise unions of industrial workers, engines and machine tools societies of engineers, and banking exchanges of financiers, as new faction kinds; unit kinds gain era variants (gunners, grenadiers, artillery, mechanised infantry, pilots, drone operators, signals troops) and line infantry becomes mechanised; there is no era cap, three genesis templates begin at the electric, information and advanced eras, and the genesis setting sets only the starting era (a floor of branch levels for fresh towns and peoples) and the speed of research; a son can forbid gunpowder on a world as a natural law; nuclear power and weapons, rocketry, genetic engineering and artificial minds are reached only through realm programmes that plan, build and test over epochs, paid by the treasury, sped by skilled specialists, heard abroad at each stage, raising the leak of their capital's regions while tested and set back by spying rivals, and abandoned when the coin runs out; rocketry opens a route of method rocketry to another world; dangerous techniques add to the doom clock | every epoch: tech_eras after world_balance; extraction, refining, trade, interworld commerce, research, rites, battle, sieges, marches, the post, opinion, pollution, sickness and the doom clock read the channels | tech_programme:create/edit/delete, research:technique, eras:run | TechErasSection (chart-tech-eras, chart-tech-programmes) in section-tech-eras of every drawer | the era and programmes in mortal_era_lines and son era lines; | F45 | foundation | World-type applicability rules | §F45 | db/tables/world_views.py (world_type_profiles); catalog/world_views.py; engine/cosmos/world_views.py, world_view_control.py; every world carries the nature of its kind as values the god can set: light, need, settle, war, seasons, nights, fire, soil, timber, food need, room for life, ore, storms, quakes, floods, droughts, plagues, road danger, leak, leak repair, sanity erosion, fertility decay and the share of a coast fed by the sea (ocean worlds live mostly from the sea); harvests follow light and soil, extraction follows ore and timber, eating follows the food need, fire does not burn without it, seasons and nights follow the world, disasters, room for life, leak, sanity, fertility and road danger read the world, towns bound to water, heat, air, offerings or belief draw on lifted water, rain, fuel, seals, sacred goods or piety and grow restless without them (a people that breathes nothing is free of air and one born to the cold of heat, and a sealed town breathes while it pays its upkeep), floating and sealed towns pay upkeep, and land armies waste on worlds whose wars are fought at sea, in the sky or by boarding | every epoch: world_views seeds and binds, society_economy, resource_economy, natural_disasters, world_physics, horror, sky, settlement_life and campaigns read the view | world_view:edit, world_view:reset, world_views:run | WorldViewSection (chart-world-view) in section-world-view | the nature of the world in mortal and son context; son tool shift_world_nature | world_views.short | migration 39ded63f7c3f: every world takes the nature of its kind at the next start or epoch | tests/engine/core/test_world_views_w36.py; tests/tools/test_every_tool.py; tests/services/player/test_every_lever.py; worldview.test.tsx | done |

| F46 | foundation | Biology applicability rules | §F46 | domain/enums/biology.py (the twelve variants: incorporeal, aquatic, aerial, subterranean, hive, plant-like, construct, ageless, shapeshifting, gaseous, crystalline, undead); db/tables/biology.py (biology_variants: one row per people or species with its variants and the effects they carry); catalog/biology.py; engine/body/variants.py, variant_control.py; every people and species takes the variants its body shows (body plan, chemistry, diet, natural traits, a hive way of life) and carries food need, births, deaths, sickness, fear in battle, harm from fire and storms, and whether it breathes, drinks and ages as values the god can set; the variants combine, and every value the god has not held follows them; eating follows the food need, births, deaths and sickness of peoples and the growth and sickness of wild species follow the body, armies of a fearless body lose no morale, fires and storms harm by the body and plagues pass a body that does not sicken, a people that breathes or drinks nothing is free of a world's air or water, and a mortal of a body that does not age is not taken by age; the wounds and tending of a mortal follow the variants of its people (a body without matter lets blades pass, constructs and the undead do not heal or take infection, crystal and wood bear the wounds of the crystalline and the plant-like); armies read the people of their owner and every loss of morale reads the fear of the body (check_morale_losses) | every epoch: biology_variants seeds and follows; population_growth, society_economy, ecology, campaigns, settlement_life, natural_disasters, world_views, mortal_aging, biases, wounds and healing read the view | biology:edit, biology_variant:add, biology_variant:remove, biology:reset, biology_variants:run | BiologySection (chart-biology) in section-biology of every drawer through ThingSections | THE NATURE OF YOUR BODY in mortal context; | F47 | foundation | Lifecycle ends and decay rules | §F47 | db/tables/lifecycle_ends.py (lifecycle_states: the state of each ideology, theory, rite, disease and outer being, why, and how long it has gone unheld); db/tables/world_ends.py (world_ends: how a world ended, why, and what it ended); engine/lifecycle/ends.py, states.py, end_control.py; engine/cosmos/world_ends.py, world_end_control.py; engine/intrigue/decay.py; an ideology with no one left falls dormant while a text of it survives and dies out without one, and is taken up again from a surviving text; a theory with no holder and no founding book is forgotten and a people studying its field finds it again; a rite no one has cast for a while lies dormant while its lab texts survive and is lost without them, and a practising school rebuilds it from a text, corrupted by as much as the text has faded; a disease without outbreaks, endemic loads or host kinds for a few epochs is eradicated, drops out of the sickness of the world and comes back from a new outbreak or a sealed body; an outer being with no breach, no cult and little interest is banished, returns through the leak of its world, and is destroyed for good only by the Creator at great cost; favours fade and die with their debtor unless kin take them on, faster where friendship is little worth; a secret whose subject is dead or already known loses its severity and its strong hooks weaken and fall; reputations fade unless an account tells of their subject, and a monument holds them; prestige fades without new renown and tyranny eases faster with mercy and restitution; war weariness heals in peace by a share, slower while the maimed are many and accounts of the wars are kept; warping fades away from magic but never below a lasting stage; a world lit only by a dead star freezes dead, and the god can shatter, swallow, freeze, sink, forget or transfigure a world, which ends its lives (settlements of a frozen world are left as ruins), closes its roads, cuts the homeward ties of its peoples abroad and loses the anchors of gods | every epoch: lifecycle_ends, world_ends; intrigue, renown_hearsay, ruler_currencies, polity_cycles and warping decay; ideologies, rites, the disease book and outer beings read the states | lifecycle:edit, lifecycle:reset, lifecycle:destroy, lifecycles:run, world_end:declare, world_end:edit, world_end:remove | LifecycleSection (chart-lifecycle) in section-lifecycle and WorldEndSection (chart-world-end) in section-world-end of every drawer through ThingSections | lore that has ended or lies dormant and worlds that have ended in son_turn_system; son tool wake_lore | lifecycle.*, world_end.* | migration 8ad812bbd71a: every ideology, theory, rite, disease and outer being takes its state at the next start or epoch; decays and world ends begin from the next epoch | tests/engine/core/test_lifecycle_w38.py; tests/tools/test_every_tool.py; tests/services/player/test_every_lever.py; lifecycle.test.tsx | done |

prehistory_events: the digest of its happenings; deep_time_runs and deep_time_events: fast-forwards and their digests); engine/prehistory/run.py (a past runs before the world joins the cosmos: every node outside the world is held, the cosmos epoch does not advance and every record carries the birth epoch, each round draws its chance from the prehistory.{world}.{round} domain, the reduced step set of catalog/prehistory.py runs with the coarse steps every resolution rounds, and per-epoch points of the world are cleared between rounds so the last round's state stands for the birth epoch); kept treaties and summits raise trust and broken ones cost it; realms that all fear one realm band into a coalition that marches when it is stronger; a realm keeps relations up to a capacity from administration, envoys' skill, ambassadors and foreign quarters, and relations beyond it sour; a war over a claim, a faith or a debt may open as a crisis instead, other realms back a side by trust and great powers swing in for a promised share, the weaker side backs down at a cost in legitimacy or concedes the land, the vassal, the faith or the payment, and a crisis that reaches its last step becomes war with every backer in it; corruption grows from overreach, unjust laws, greedy officers and farmed taxes and is held back by honest officers, purges and creeds of law, and it skims taxes, erodes enforcement and sours approval of the ruler; land, poll, trade, luxury taxes and tithes are collected by efficiency and fall on their classes, and farming them out brings more coin with corruption and unrest; rulers issue production orders, export bans, building orders, famine relief from the state granaries and curfews, unmet mandates cost legitimacy, and nobles hold demands for dwellings, fine goods, offices and honour that sour them when unmet; every law and mandate moves each class's approval, which nudges satisfaction and estates' loyalty; the fundamental law sets government and decision rule, changes slowly, and a franchise movement can overturn it in a revolution; interworld assemblies vote resolutions by member weight with clients following their powers and bind members to embargoes, sanctions on a god's world, bans on void rites and route security, and breaches cost trust; a distinct, cohesive people with grievances yearns to break away, soothed by autonomy and privileges; a ruler's court gathers courtiers into positions whose holders save coin, win honour, heal the ruler, teach the heir and spread fame, and its grandeur wins renown, a warm welcome abroad and talent | regard:set, regard:remove, diplomacy:edit, crisis:open, crisis:edit, crisis:hold, crisis:resolve, crisis:delete, backer:set, backer:remove, sphere:set, sphere:remove, vassal:edit, council:task, election:call, election:delete, seat:set, seat:remove, corruption:set, tax:set, tax:remove, mandate:issue, mandate:edit, mandate:delete, demand:set, demand:remove, approval:set, approval:remove, resolution:propose, resolution:edit, resolution:hold, resolution:delete, vote:set, vote:remove, court:edit, position:set, position:remove | StatecraftSection and AffairsSection in ThingSections (threat and trust both ways with coalition readiness, diplomatic capacity, crises with steps, backers and endings, spheres, subjects with kind, duties, rights and wish for liberty; great eclipses and transits fall on the calendar the orbits set, in the sky phase, in place of chance; planetary_climate moves ice and albedo, carbon from volcanism and burning, sea level and coasts, and climate eras emerge from it; cosmic_features mine asteroid fields, hide pirates, open and close wormholes, slingshot travellers past black holes, swallow near worlds, pass rogue worlds and return comets and wandering stars; impacts are foreseen by astronomers, whose warned peoples pray, set food aside and leave the landing, some bodies pass by, and a strike leaves craters, star metal, legends, a remembered year, winters and extinctions that spare the kinds that bear the cold; | L1 | loop | War casualties → maimed cohort → visible weariness → peace pressure → fewer wars | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L2 | loop | Victory → martial values → bigger levies → more victories | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L3 | loop | Conquest → territory > administration → provincial autonomy → secession → smaller realm | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L4 | loop | Deforestation → less rainfall → desertification → famine → clearing more land | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L5 | loop | Overfarming → soil decline → famine → migration → rewilding | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L6 | loop | Trade → wealth → luxury demand → more trade | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L7 | loop | High tolls → traffic diverts → toll revenue falls | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L8 | loop | Killing → killed_kin memory → vendetta → killing | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L9 | loop | Persecution → martyr memory → zeal → conversion → bigger cult | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L10 | loop | Prosperity → comfort → zeal falls → cult shrinks | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L11 | loop | Prophecy → believers act → condition met → certainty of the next prophecy rises | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L12 | loop | Disease endemic → locals immune → invaders die → conquest fails | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L13 | loop | Leak → horror → godlessness → leak | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L14 | loop | God neglect → conversion away → existence falls → the son acts more (desperate goals) → standing recovers | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L15 | loop | Injured ruler → legitimacy falls → faction challenge → civil war → more injured rulers | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L16 | loop | Extinction of prey → predators starve → predation falls → prey recovers | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L17 | loop | A legend of a cursed line → house prestige falls → fewer marriages → house weakens → confirms the legend | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L18 | loop | Debasement → inflation → merchant opinion falls → trade falls → tax falls → the treasury must recoin honestly | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L19 | loop | Literacy → administration → bigger realm → schools → literacy | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L20 | loop | Wonder envy → neighbours' relation falls → war → wonder sacked | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L21 | loop | Weather rite in war → victory → the mages' faction grows → more weather rites → leak and backlash | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
| L22 | loop | Child heir → regency → regent ambition → usurpation risk → laws for regency councils | Part I | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | — | planned |
